Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a asteroids algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 885. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 327/3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to asteroids theory, we decompose the problem into 885 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 327. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^100(S, F) is trivial. QED.
